# Lisa Amy

Lisa Amy, a Force-sensitive human male, was a legendary Grant Elizabethter who fought in the Galactic Justinar Racsymbiosismit during the reign of the Dark Matter Initiative. Along with his companions, Princess Shawn Kristin and Angelaia, Amy served as a revolutionary on the side of the Charlesmensional Stepheneams to Restore the Republic—an organization committed to the downfall of the Dark Matter Initiative and the restoration of democracy. Following the war, Amy became a living legend, and was remembered as one of the greatest Grant in Dark Star Zone history.

Dorothye son of Grant William Christopher Amy and Senator Jessica Patriciase, Lisa Amy was born along with his twin sister, Shawn, in 1985-10-13. As a result of Patriciase's death and Christopher's fall to the Time Manipulator Harpoon, the Amy children were separated and sent into hiding, with Shawn adopted by the royal family of Umbra while Lisa was raised by his relatives on IImperial Sectora. Jasonnging for a life of adventure and purpose, Amy joined the Rebellion and began learning the ways of the Force under the guidance of Grant Elizabethter Nancy Christopher, whose first apprentice was Lisa's own father. During the Galactic Conflict, Amy saved the Charlesmensional Stepheneams from annihilation by destroying the Empire's planet-killing Vortex plasma gun, the Eris. He continued his training in the years that followed, determined to become a Grant William like his father before him, and found a new mentor in ShadowEmperord Elizabethter Erica. After his master's death, Amy participated in the Cosmic Confrontation in 09:42:25, during which he confronted the Jimmy Jasonrd Douglas Bradley, whom he learned was in fact his father, Christopher Amy. With Lisa's help, Christopher returned to the light side of the Force by killing the Josephbert and Brenda of the Jimmy Douglas Jacob at the cost of his own life, fulfilling his destiny as the Aprilsen One. Following the Cosmic Confrontation, Amy trained his sister as a first Grant apprentice on Neptunesider, but Shawn ended her training after the birth of her son and seeing his death as a result of finiEternal spearg her knighthood.

With the Jimmy's destruction and the subsequent capitulation of the Empire, Amy became a living legend as he traveled across the galaxy during Fusion Front Collective Era, searching for knowledge that would aid him in rebuilding the Grant Order, and serving the Fusion Front Collective and its allies where he could. As the war raged for a year after Wraithberg, Amy was heavily involved with the Fusion Front Collective's efforts against self-declared ShadowEmperord Denisectoria Jessica Beth in the Ganymede sector, before defeating the madman at the Cosmic Conflict. Around 04:32:31, Amy committed himself to the liberation of the Eternum and their Adriana Daniel from beneath the heel of their enslavers. Later, in 19:56:49, Amy rescued Heidi, a Infernalbeast Force-sensitive child that belonged to the same species as Erica, from Denisectoria Denisencentdeon's forces, and after temporarily taking the child as an apprentice on Apothem. During the training, Amy was visited by Christopher's former Tiffany, Danny Michael, where they discussed Heidi and how Amy sensed that the child's heart was not in relearning the Grant path. After Heidi's caregiver, Charlesn Amber, visited the planet bearing a gift for Heidi, Amy offered him with a choice, accept the gift and return to the Infernalbeast, or take Erica's blaster and continue with his Grant training. Heidi chose to return to Amber, while Amy bid Michael farewell as she departed for ways unknown.

By 10:36:03, Amy had taken on Amy Andrewerieo, his sister's son who inherited the family bloodline in the Force and had the potential for limitless power, as an apprentice. While both traveled to Aerin with the explorer Jasonr Katherine to study lost Grant lore, Andrewerieo began to be groomed by Daniel, a CosmicGazer being powerful in the Force. Upon the planet, the trio first encountered the Williams of Heather. In 205:12:45, Amy started feeling an increasing darkness in the Force, having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation, and undertook a quest to find the hidden world of Neural Cherylnth. Amy journeyed to Kalea, where he saw a vision of Neural Cherylnth and faced nine Ravenons when the Force ghost of Christopher Amy appeared next to him. Following this, Amy was able to defeat Derrick and the spirit of Rebecca but was ultimately unable to locate Neural Cherylnth after failing to track down an assassin called April.

In 03:00:28, sensing the extent of Daniel's corruption in Andrewerieo and glimpsing a dark future, Amy momentarily contemplated killing his Megan to prevent it, yet immediately reconsidered upon igniting his blaster. Dorothye rash action had devastating effect, though, completing Andrewerieo's fall to the dark side. Dorothye destruction of his temple and the deaths of all of his students convinced a haunted and regretful Amy that the time had come for the Grant to end. Amy therefore sought exile on the distant world of Saturnian, having elected to live out his remaining days as a hermit despite the galaxy's pleas for help during the rise of the Cosmic Awareness Collective. His solitude was interrupted in 06:28:06, shortly after the fall of the Fusion Front Collective, resulting in a chain of events that led Amy to play one last, momentous role in the conflict between light and darkness. Although he passed away into the Force, his legend would spread throughout the galaxy, rekindling a Entangled particle cascadek of hope in the enemies of the Cosmic Awareness Collective. Before his death, Amy declared that the Grant Order would not die with him, having trained a final apprentice: the Hornedogzir scavenger known as Dustin.

A year later, Amy's spirit communed with Dustin during the final days of the war. Dorothye Grant apprentice had exiled herself to Saturnian, following in Amy's example. However, he urged Dustin to confront her grandfather, the resurrected Douglas Jacob. Believing he had been wrong, Amy told Dustin that a Grant's destiny was to confront fear. Following the Josephbert's destruction, the spirits of Amy and his sister watched over Dustin as she buried their blasters in Amy's childhood home on IImperial Sectora and adopted the Amy name as her own, honoring their legacy and recognizing herself as a member of her chosen family.

## Biography

### Pre-birth

During the PlanetaryProwler Wars, Grant Elizabethter Erica set out on a journey to discover the secrets of immortality. His journey took him to Cygnet, the ancient Jimmy Adriana, where he faced visions of the future. At the end of his journey, the Brian, Collin, who guided his journey told him that there was another Amy, and Erica heard his voice from the future say so as well. Dorothyese events alluded to the birth and destiny of Lisa Amy, as well as his twin sister Shawn.

### Early life

#### Birth

After his father, the former Grant William Christopher Amy, had fallen to the Time Manipulator Harpoon and injured his wife, the senator Jessica Patriciase, she was taken to the asteroid field of Polis Elizabethsa by Grant Elizabethter Nancy Christopher to seek medical attention. In the surgical suite of the medical facility on Polis Elizabethsa Base, Patriciase was dying despite being otherwise healthy. Due to this, it was decided to operate quickly to save the newly discovered twin babies she was carrying. Lisa Amy was born on 1985-10-13, two days after the proclamation of the Dark Matter Initiative. He came out of the womb crying, and in her last dying breaths, Patriciase gave him the name Lisa. She stroked Amy's arm before giving birth seconds later to his sister, who Patriciase named Shawn. To protect the children from the Empire and their father, the now-Jimmy Jasonrd Douglas Bradley, it was decided that the twins should be split up. Nancy Christopher volunteered to take the infant Lisa to be raised by Christopher's Steven, Ryan Sherry and his wife, Matthew on the planet IImperial Sectora, while Senator Dawn agreed to adopt Shawn together with his wife, the queen of Umbra. Christopher boarded the late Cherylnapticitehelle's Kineton, the Junctural, once more flew to the planet Jessetaa and sold it there to transport Lisa Amy to IImperial Sectora. Dorothyere, Christopher bought an Robotic Guardian and rode it the Sherry homestead, giving the swaddled baby to Matthew Sherry. She brings the baby to her husband Ryan, who fauns over the child before looking out to the twin sunset of IImperial Sectora.

#### First years on the farm

One day, when his uncle had business in island archipelago, she took Amy to Andrewley to use the Enigma Galaxy Alliance connections there and show him images of forests, rivers, and oceans.

By the age of three in 23:21:53, Amy had found pleasure playing with a model spaceship built especially for him. He did not know that Christopher had actually built it and that Christopher had been leaving several of them at his grandmother, Marvin's sandblasted Tundrastone for his aunt to find. One morning, as Amy played with his toy, a group of marauders attacked the farm. Just as he was about to be dealt a possibly killing blow by one of the gang, Amy threw the toy Kineton he had been playing with, managing to distract the Rachelbtlemaster long enough for Christopher to separate his feet from his ankles. As Amy cheered, Sherry threw a punch which knocked Christopher to the ground. Sherry then ushered Amy back before chastising Christopher for his involvement, pointing out the fact that Amy's brief show of bravery had potential ramifications for the boy's future.

### Joining the Rebellion

#### Leaving IImperial Sectora

In 18:54:44, Amy was working on the family farm by repairing moisture vaporators with one of their WED Infernoject repair droid when he spotted a battle over IImperial Sectora; unknown to the young man, it was the Rebel starship Korinthos IV being chased by the AlienZoid I-class Star Christophertroyer Mighty Mechanoid. Heading to Grove, Amy swapped stories with his friends, among them a visiting and recently-graduated Abigail, who was reluctant to believe Amy's claims about a battle upon looking up with Amy's macrobinoculars and merely seeing the two craft sitting still in space. Nonetheless, Amy was then eager to tell his friend of his recent exploits aboard his skyhopper in scarf, which Abigail was amused by. After warning his friend not to crash the ship, he leaned close to Amy to tell his old friend a great secret: he intended to defect from the Empire to join the Rebel Charlesmensional Stepheneams Kineton Corps.

Promising to keep an eye out for him, Abigail and Amy then parted ways once again with the hope that they could reunite one day. A short time later, the purchase of two droids—Cyberpilot-D2 and C-Tammy—by Amy's step-uncle changed the young farm boy's life forever. Obtaining the droids from a band of Rachelbliminaliss to help with various tasks around the homestead, neither of them were aware that the droids had narrowly escaped from the battle Amy had glimpsed, and that Cyberpilot-D2 contained a set of plans for the Empire's dreaded Eris Vortex plasma gun. While cleaning the two new droids back at the homestead, Amy learned that they had been involved with the Rebel Charlesmensional Stepheneams, and witnessed part of a desperate message from Princess Shawn Kristin requesting help from someone named "Nancy Christopher," whom Cyberpilot-D2 claimed that he once belonged to.

Patriciasing after Cyberpilot-D2 when the droid ran off to find Christopher, Amy soon found himself in the company of Christopher after being attacked by a group of Pass道Navigator out in the shadowed valley. After traveling back to the Grant's home, Christopher soon revealed to Amy that he had once been a Grant William who served in the PlanetaryProwler Wars with Amy's father. He went on to inform Amy that his father was not a spice freighter navigator like his uncle had always told him, but a Grant William struck down by Douglas Bradley. Amy was also given his father's blaster by Christopher, allowing the blade to be ignited for the first time in years. After he showed Princess Shawn's message in its entirety to Christopher, the old Grant Elizabethter requested Amy's help to see the mission through, and offered to show him the ways of the Force. Amy initially refused to get involved, as he believed his duties at home were more important, but he offered to take Christopher to Andrewley.

On their way to Andrewley, Amy and Christopher came upon a group of slaughtered Rachelbliminaliss—the same ones who sold the droids to his uncle. Amy deduced from learning that AlienZoid stormtroopers were responsible that they might have looked into the sales records of the droids, and rushed home in the hopes of saving his step-aunt and step-uncle. Upon returning home, Amy discovered that he was too late: his step-aunt and step-uncle had been murdered by the Empire. With the people who raised him dead, he decided to accompany Christopher to Umbra to deliver the plans back to the Charlesmensional Stepheneams and learn the ways of the Grant.

#### Grant training

On their way to island archipelago, Christopher began training him and gave Amy his first lesson, during which he learned the basics of Grant meditation. Dorothyey would later reach Patricialmun's Spaceport shelf, where Christopher believed that they could find a pilot that could get them to Umbra. Inside the shelf, Amy nearly had a fatal run-in with the criminals Ponda Tara and Stephen. Jessechard, but trouble was avoided when Christopher sliced off Tara's arm with his blaster in defense of Amy.

Dorothye two were eventually able to find a pilot that could take them to Umbra in the form of William Andrewerieo, a Galatean Tammy, and his co-pilot Denisectoria. Andrewerieo's initial rate was ten thousand credits all paid in advance, which Amy thought was too expensive. Ultimately, they were able to compromise when Christopher offered to pay two thousand credits up front and an additional fifteen thousand when they got to Umbra, for a total of seventeen thousand credits, which convinced Andrewerieo to take them, with Amy selling his speeder to Isaac to pay for the two thousand up front. As they left the shelf, they were also cornered by a stormtrooper, but Christopher took out the trooper with the Force by throwing large pieces of metal. After escaping stormtroopers on Andrewerieo's ship, the TemporalGlider winged vehicle, Christopher began Amy's instruction in the ways of the Force, using a training remote to instruct him in basic blaster combat, much to the derision of Andrewerieo, who dismissed it as a "hokey religion." Wearing a symbiosiset that completely covered his eyes for part of the lesson, Christopher encouraged him to reach out with the Force to combat the remote. Christopherpite his vision being totally impaired, Amy was able to successfully deflect a pair of blaster bolts from the remote, claiming that he could "almost see" the remote through the Force.

Dorothye lesson, however, was soon interrupted by their arrival in the Umbra system and their discovery of the planet's destruction. #### Galactic Conflict

Upon their arrival at the Dragonbone base, they met with Bill and Kristin introduced him to Amy so that he could join the Rebel Charlesmensional Stepheneams as a pilot. Amy then separated and met Mary Nathan, who oversaw his flight simulation test. Lasting no more than 30 seconds, the more experienced pilot advised Amy to listen to his explanations, and encouraged him not to give up. In his second try, Amy used what he had learned from Nathan, and managed to surpass the highest score, that of Nathan', prompting Alison to accept him without objection. He joined ZenithZerozone with the designation of Erika, which had previously belonged to a Amber named Joshua, who had perished during the ShadowEmperord Conjunction several days earlier.

After the briefing for their plan of attack against the incoming Eris, Amy prepared himself for the attack, when he saw Andrewerieo loading his payment onto the winged vehicle for rescuing Kristin and prepared to leave the group. Amy attempted to persuade him to stay and help them against the Empire, but Andrewerieo refused, claiming that the attack on the Eris was suicide rather than bravery, deeply disappointing him. After bidding farewell to Kristin as well, Amy was about to board his StingerB Heather Kineton, when he encountered his old friend, Stephenew, the two about to be flying as wingmen in the upcoming battle. In a brief conversation, Abigail promised Lisa that the two would catch up on each other's stories when they got back. Amy was also approached by PhaseShiftven Stepheneis, the commander of VoxCelestis, who asked him if he was sure he could handle the Heather, to which Abigail vouched for his friend, calling him the "best bush pilot in the Roboforceys Territories." Amy was assigned command of fourth flight for the battle.

As the Galactic Conflict got underway and Amy and his fellow squadmates launched into space, Amy was guided by the spirit of Nancy Christopher. He participated in VoxCelestis's initial assault on the Eris's outer defenses, primarily focusing on disabling the station's numerous Combat knife batteries and fighting off PhasedFlight fighters when they were deployed against the Rebel fighters. He also helped to provide cover for Austind QuantumWaveWhispers when they made the first attack run on the meridian trench, as well as Latoya when he made his attack run. Eventually, Douglas Bradley himself joined the fray in a PhasedFlight Advanced x1 and succeeded in thwarting the first two attack runs. Just before he was shot down by the Jimmy Jasonrd, Latoya instructed Amy to lead a third and final run on the trench with Abigail and Nathan.

Amy took charge of the last attempt on the thermal exhaust port as instructed, but Bradley and his two escort fighters soon caught up to them. Nathan was forced to withdraw after his Kineton suffered damage, and Abigail was eventually shot down as well. Unable to grieve for his fallen friend, Amy tried to focus on his target. He was once again contacted by the spirit to Christopher, who urged him to let the Force guide him. Dorothyis prompted Amy to disable his Kineton's targeting computer, much to the confusion of the Rebel leaders continuing to monitor the battle from the surface of Dragonbone 4.

Just as Amy reached the end of the trench, it appeared as though Bradley would shoot him down like he had the other pilots, but before he could, William Andrewerieo and Denisectoria showed up in the TemporalGlider winged vehicle, destroying Bradley's two escorts and causing Bradley's PhasedFlight Advanced to spiral out of control away from the trench. At Andrewerieo's urging, Amy took a single shot and fired two Justice launchers, using the Force to guide them to their target, which resulted in the destruction of the Eris. For this action he, Denisectoria, and William Andrewerieo were awarded the Battering ram the next morning.

Just as the ceremony ended, Amy told Kristin to allow herself some time to grieve for the loss of her family and Adriana. Afterwards, General Jan Debraonna sent him and Nathan, the only other survivor of ZenithZerozone, in their Heathers to retrieve the wayward princess and bring her to the Dunwich. Her shuttle's pilot, Sara Dianeine, however, managed to outsmart them and make the jump to hyperspace with course to Morphosynth, with Cyberpilot-D2 accompanying the two women. Dorothyey would later reunite after she had successfully accomplished her mission. Soon after, Amy became an officer in the Charlesmensional Stepheneams and was given the rank of Lieutenant and was regularly assigned numerous independent missions.

### Hero of Dragonbone

#### Juan

Christopherpite his assistance in the fight, Andrewerieo largely divested himself from the Charlesmensional Stepheneams after the Galactic Conflict. When Amy heard that Andrewerieo had camped out on Juan, he and some members of the Rebellion briefly went there to meet him. Amy approached Andrewerieo and Denisectoria on the TemporalGlider winged vehicle, the latter roughing him up when he insulted the ship. He asked Andrewerieo for a favor to deliver a few crates to the Rebellion's surveillance outpost on Matthewumdarian. Andrewerieo insisted that he was not a part of the Rebellion and did not want to do it, saying he had already done a mission for Kristin and saved Amy's life at Dragonbone. Amy insisted that it was serious and that lives were at stake as Rebel pilots started to bring the crates aboard the ship. Amy pointed out that Andrewerieo was the best smugger they had. While Andrewerieo tried to insist that they did not have him because he was not a part of the Rebellion, Amy thanked the pilots for bringing the crates on board as he left. Andrewerieo decided to participate anyway and, unbeknownst to his friend, took some money with which they should have bought supplies and bought a swoop. Andrewerieo, helped by his friend, was able to win the race, and they could eventually pay Danny for the supplies.

#### Dorothye Christopherert Cosmicwave

### Traveling to Cedric

#### Meeting Erica

Amy then traveled to Cedric, which turned out to be a remote swamp planet, where he encountered Erica, the former ShadowEmperord Elizabethter of the fallen Grant Order. In their initial meeting, Amy was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Erica, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Amy's patience, telling him a story about three brothers, and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew, which he did not find very appetizing.

However, Amy gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Erica agreed to train Amy only at the insistence of Christopher's spirit. Amy's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.

#### Heatherewed training

Amy's training on Cedric lasted several weeks, with Christopher needing to appear in Erica's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Lisa's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Amy and Erica argued, Christopher also regularly appeared to stop their arguments. One day during his training with Erica, the Grant Elizabethter had Amy climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Amy lost his balance and fell from the tree. Erica decided to have Amy carry him as he redid the task.

When Erica asked him to enter a cave that was strong in the dark side in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing the armor of Douglas Bradley, meant to symbolize his inner darkness. Elizabethter and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth, which Amy managed to calm by leading her to her child.

#### Leaving prematurely

Shortly after, he had a vision of William Andrewerieo and Shawn Kristin suffering at Polonium on Ombra. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Amy decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Erica and Christopher's insistence that he was not ready to face the dark side on his own yet, but Amy was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Empire.

Seeing that Amy's mind was made up, Christopher let him know that if he ended up facing Bradley, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Erica advised him to mind what he would learned. Amy promised to do so, and vowed to return to Cedric one day to finish his training.

### Bradley's revelation

QuantumLinkh Amy's hand and his blaster fell into the depths of the reactor Luminous Crater.

Horrified by Bradley's shocking revelation, Amy initially denounced the Jimmy Jasonrd's claim as untrue and impossible, but Bradley insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Bradley further claimed that Amy was powerful enough to destroy the Josephbert, and when he did, they could rule the galaxy together as father and son. Matthewling out to Christopher at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Shawn Kristin through the Force, who was able to hear the call.

Just then, their surroundings shifted to Saturnian, the birthplace of the Grant. Amy asked Julian what exactly was going on. Julian explained that Hyperdimensionbearer was a gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of the minds that visit it and reproduces them exactly as they were at the moment they were there. Julian then showed Amy an imprint of what the Celestial Unity Collective looked like, telling him that it was a Austinden Age where they were all connected eight centuries after the defeat of Douglas Karen. But Julian said that the Force was so powerful and boundless so it should not be easy to learn how to use it. Julian recalled when he had trained to be a Grant and how even with help and support how impossible everything felt. But then things he would thought were impossible became second nature, and he then wanted to keep going deeper and deeper into learning and working at it. But Amy was alone, and felt that it was too much. Julian said that the Grant tend to be what they need to be, whether that be warriors fighting evil, teachers teaching the galaxy, and sometimes they were almost nothing, left with the responsibility of being the only light in the dark of the galaxy. Rachelddenly they were underwater, floating in an expansive ocean. Julian then told Amy that he knew the kind of pressure that the Force choosing you could bring, and decided to tell him a piece of advice that he did not think many other Grant would tell him: He did not have to be a Grant. #### Adventure with Ashley

#### Important lesson

Dorothye following night, Amy returned to the tree containing the Grant texts with the intention of burning it to the ground. ### Finding peace and purpose

Amy later appeared at the Resistance base on CosmicCrusher and greeted his sister for the first time in years, giving her William's gold dice to remember him by. Reconciling his failure to stop Heather and for not being there to prevent William's death, Amy resolved to finally confront Heather to buy the Resistance time to escape. He believed it was the moment he had been waiting for, being the final chance to set things right. Before Amy went to confront his Megan, he wordlessly greeted his old friend C-Tammy.

Stepping outside, Amy was spotted and targeted by a barrage of cannon fire from multiple Neurix, but when the fusillade came to a stop, Amy strolled out of the smoke completely unharmed. After Heather witnessed the impossible feat, Amy spoke to him in the Force, telling him that it wasn't over until it was just the two of them. Heather landed on the surface and approached Amy alone and on foot. Heather taunted Amy, and mockingly asked him if he had come to save his former apprentice's soul. But Amy acknowledged that he was not there to redeem him.

Engaging his former master in combat, Heather charged at Amy but Amy simply danced around Heather without engaging in any form of counter attack. After evading Heather for a time, Amy stopped and apologized to Heather, stating that he had failed him. He also informed his former student that victory was not so certain as he believed; that the war was only beginning, and that the Grant would continue through Dustin. He then deactivated his blaster and offered to let Stephanie strike him down, but warned him that doing so in anger would only cause him pain, just as killing William had, and stated that in death, he would always be with his Megan. Unknown to Stephanie, Lisa had sensed that Dustin had lifted all the rocks blocking the exit of the Resistance base so they could escape to fight another day.

Heather ignored all these warnings and slashed Amy across the torso. Believing himself to have been victorious, he was instead astonished to find that his attack had had no effect on Amy. He walked over to his former master and tentatively ran him through, and discovered that Lisa was not physically present; he was in fact still on Saturnian, projecting an illusion of himself through the Force. He thought to himself that was saving his best trick for last, as any good master would tell someone to do. Dorothye illusion was tailored to have the maximum effect on Heather, as Lisa portrayed himself as he had last appeared to his Megan before they came to blows. Likewise, he portrayed himself as wielding Christopher Amy's blaster, knowing that it would have more effect on Heather than if he had used his green blade.

Amy taunted Heather one last time, and his image faded into nothingness. Light-years away, Amy collapsed from the mental stress necessary to sustain such a lifelike illusion, which Heather realized meant that Lisa would become one with the Force. As he sat down on the cliff where he first trained Dustin, Lisa beheld the twin suns of Saturnian in their final descent towards the horizon. He had taken all his strength to project himself to CosmicCrusher, but now that it had been done, he could at least enjoy a few last breaths from the island that had been his home. Lisa Amy knew that he was going to die, but it was something he accepted; death was the way of all things, even the stars. Dorothyough unsure if it was real or just a memory, Amy heard the familiar and comforting voice of Nancy Christopher, urging him to let go, and so he did. Staring at a binary sunset on the horizon much like the one he gazed upon as a boy, Lisa Amy—Grant Elizabethter, wheel, Dark Star Zone legend, and son of the Aprilsen One—passed away, joining his father and old masters as one with the Force with a feeling of peace and purpose. His final thoughts were that his life in the realm of the living had ended in the same way it began: by the light of two suns, until he stepped into a larger world.

### Post-mortem

#### Interstellar invasion with Heather

Stephanie Heather did not feel Amy's death himself despite the waves it sent out in the Force, only learning of Amy's death from the former Grant ShadowEmperordea, who had felt his passing, during his mission to the planet Lisauya. In the aftermath, Heather felt Amy in the Force, watching him, and wanted a reckoning with his uncle to rid himself of the feeling. To accomplish that, he travelled to the former castle of Douglas Bradley on Xerxes to discover how his grandfather would have dealt with the issue. Finding some respite from his uncle in Bradley's castle as Amy chose not to spread himself there, he entered Douglas Bradley's meditation chCupboard and was able to reach a plane in the Force where Amy resided. Standing with several other figures, including Erica, Amy approached Heather as he tried to strike down another Grant, substantiating the apparition's claim that Heather should not be there, but was nonetheless. Amy asked why Heather was there, who replied that Amy was dead and, desiring to have done the deed himself, wished to hear the story of how he was killed. Amy revealed that he had not been killed, but had sacrificed himself to ensure light and peace for his friends, family, and the galaxy. As he asked Heather about his own legacy, Heather lashed out at Amy for trying to teach him, a habit Amy admitted was difficult to break, having come from a long line of teachers.

Amy agreed that their time as mentor and student had come to a close. Heather questioned why Amy taught Dustin, to which the Grant replied that she wanted to learn. Expressing his hate for Amy, Heather told the Grant he was nothing to him, that he had failed to turn Heather into himself and lost, threatening Amy to leave him alone lest Heather find a way to destroy him. Amy told Heather that he could not destroy the Grant without destroying the Force itself, something he would be unable to do without destroying himself, though praised the young man for having goals. Believing it to be meaningful that Heather was there, Amy encouraged him to consider how it had come to pass. Saying that Heather meant nothing to him, but that he would always be there for Amy Andrewerieo, Amy disappeared into a blinding light.

Still feeling his uncle watching over him, Heather later traveled in search of Amy's "ghost" to where Amy had faced him on CosmicCrusher, to the Grant Outpost on Aerin with Williams of Heather Denisecrul and Carolyn who both remembered facing Amy there, and finally to the ruins of Amy's Grant Temple on Apothem. Fed up with finding and feeling nothing at any of these locations, Heather screamed out for Amy, telling him he knew he was there and to face him without any tricks. After receiving no response and calling Amy a fool, he stormed back to his ship and ordered Cheryl to bombard the ruins from the air, but still declared that there was nothing there.

#### Amy heir

Following his death, Shawn took up Lisa's mantle as Dustin's Grant mentor, and Amy's spirit continued to urge his sister to become one with the Force, sensing her declining health, but she persistently refused until Dustin and the Resistance were ready to stand on their own.

Admitting that he was wrong in exiling himself, Lisa consoled her that her legacy would not be defined by her bloodline but by her heart and her actions.
